A02 FOUNDATIONAL TEST: REPORT (TEST-ONLY review evidence)

What this is: the isolated A02 engineering experiment Christian authorized, "I authorize the next bounded, isolated A02 engineering experiment." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026): Steps 1 and 2 of the review pathway, run by the handoff and consumed by the checked configuration chain through a TEST-ONLY option no route passes; High Press Escape's approved identity and scoring rows restated by us as CANDIDATE; the reasoning-disconnection safeguards; the zero-, one- and two-manipulation runs; the earliest consequential blocker; a brief A02/TA02 comparison.
Standing: TEST-ONLY. Isolated: nothing a coach or an administrator can reach runs it. Not deployed. Not merged. Not an authorization, not approved wording, not a selection input. Engineering evidence only: no run here establishes ecological suitability or representative sufficiency. "This authorization is for the isolated development pathway. It does not authorize production changes or broader Stage 2 implementation." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026)
Dates: Christian's reply about A02 was pasted on 10 November 2026 without its mail header, so its received date is not recorded; every citation of it is dated 'reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026', its paste date, never a received date. Every other reply is cited by its received date. The chain's own sentences cite one reply by our old filing date: 'reply filed 30 October 2026' is Christian's second reply received 27 October 2026.
Quotation marks: double, Christian's words, verbatim, with the reply's date (typographic ones where the words themselves hold a straight one); single, ours (values, labels, engine sentences). Inside an engine line, the text after 'Christian, <date>:' is the knowledge item's stored evidence, close to Christian's words but not checked here as verbatim.
How each item is told, as Christian asked, "Please distinguish implemented and tested behavior from proposals, assumptions, and ecological suitability." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026): IMPLEMENTED AND TESTED (what the code does, measured by a registered test of this build); OPEN (unresolved, or detected as an open disconnection: never a pass); PROPOSALS (ours, for Christian's decision); ASSUMPTIONS (ours, each labelled where it enters); ECOLOGICAL SUITABILITY (not established by any run).

FIRST, FOR CHRISTIAN'S DECISION: THE CONSTRAINT SELECTOR
- To bypass the numerical quota in the isolated experiment, the constraint selector's source file (generateSelection.ts) gained one TEST-ONLY parameter, a switch that is off unless a caller passes it, and since stage W2 (11 November 2026) a second, the declared treatment FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS, honoured only with the switch, in 14 places (each held to the file's text by this report's test):
   generateSelection.ts:662-667: the bounded search over constraints takes whether to search the pool whole, false unless the switch is on (stage W2)
   generateSelection.ts:685: searched whole, its cap of 2 per role is not applied
   generateSelection.ts:704-705: the size rule's function takes the authorized set's own size, null unless the switch is on
   generateSelection.ts:718-721: given that size, the size rule admits exactly it instead of 2 to 4 (the first of the two places the size rule lives; the set whole, stage W2)
   generateSelection.ts:787-790: the enumeration takes the list of sizes it tries, and the switch's checks (stage W2)
   generateSelection.ts:803: the enumeration reads them
   generateSelection.ts:836: the enumeration searches the record's pool whole under the switch
   generateSelection.ts:840: the enumeration tries the listed sizes instead of the fixed 2, 3 and 4 (the second place)
   generateSelection.ts:852-853: the package check receives the switch's options: every selected member validated and, under its treatment, no foundation presence; nothing without the switch
   generateSelection.ts:891-906: the selector takes the switch, off unless a caller passes it, and (stage W2) the treatment FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS, off unless a caller passes it
   generateSelection.ts:1040-1048: the switch is honoured only for an evaluated authorization record and throws for any other pool; under it the only size tried is the set's own, and the switch's checks are made (the treatment refused without the switch)
   generateSelection.ts:1072-1073: the sizes and the switch's checks are handed to the enumeration
   generateSelection.ts:1095: the own size is handed to the size rule
   generateSelection.ts:1213-1232: what the switch adds (the set whole, the pool whole, every selected member validated) and the treatment (no foundation presence), and the treatment refused without the switch (stage W2)
- Since stage W2, the selector's package check and the package builder and validator it calls gained TEST-ONLY options the selector passes only under the switch, in 11 places (each held to its file's text; only the package check passes them):
   selection-compatibility.ts:25-41: the package check takes two TEST-ONLY options, every selected member validated and no foundation presence, and names the words of the tightening's refusal
   selection-compatibility.ts:110-123: the treatment only with the tightening (refused otherwise); under it the package built and validated without the foundation clause, then every member's presence checked
   selection-compatibility.ts:127-128: without the treatment, under the switch only, every member's presence checked after the package's own checks
   selection-compatibility.ts:137-145: the tightening: a selected constraint absent from the checked package is refused, named
   build-constraint-package.ts:349-350: the exchange builder tolerates an absent foundation (read only into the overlay's list, which skips it)
   build-constraint-package.ts:606-629: the treatment's type and the builder's TEST-ONLY fourth parameter; three arguments return the package as before
   build-constraint-package.ts:635: the foundation clause, not applied under the treatment
   build-constraint-package.ts:651-654: the shaping and consequence picks exclude the foundation only where one was picked
   validate-constraint-package.ts:2: a type-only import of the treatment's types
   validate-constraint-package.ts:302-313: the validator's TEST-ONLY fourth parameter; three arguments validate as before
   validate-constraint-package.ts:333: the foundation clause, not applied under the treatment; every other check unchanged
- Since stage W1 (b) (11 November 2026), to take the review pathway's K2 result in place of a per-goal authorization record, the same file reads the pool's authority from the hint and admits K2's result only as the empty set, in 4 places (each held to the file's text; production never hands it that authority):
   generateSelection.ts:152: the pool's authority is read from the hint (AUTHORIZATION_RECORD for every production hint), so K2's is never relabelled
   generateSelection.ts:168: the pool's source likewise
   generateSelection.ts:1039: the zero branch admits K2's result, as an empty set only (the quota bypass stays record-only, :1043)
   generateSelection.ts:1198-1211: the guard: false for every other pool, true for K2's empty set, and it throws for a K2 pool with any candidate (K2 authorizes no manipulation)
- No route, controller or service passes them (a registered static test reads every non-test source file). With them off, the selector's behaviour is compared with main's by the behaviour snapshot, which the evidence folder's regression record holds (recorded on 96944b3).
- Question: is a TEST-ONLY parameter that no route passes within "I am not authorizing a production change to the constraint selector at this stage." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026)? It is removable: deleting the 14 places and the package check's 11 returns the files to main's text. The lone Neutral Player runs of item 4 (S4-NP, S4-NP@A02-D, S4-NP~reversed, S4-NP~neutral) would then change outcome, refused by the size rule as before (S4-NP/no-bypass: refused at selection); the other lone sets, and the sets with no foundation member, would be refused by the size rule or the foundation clause instead of by the kept checks; a set of two or three would again be enumerated with its subsets of 2 or more, its pool capped at 2 per role, a member absent from the checked package unchecked (Neutral Player with Central Density Condition, refused by MEMBER_PRESENCE with the switch, is admitted without it by the production check: item 4); and Neutral Player with Wide Zone Advantage, a set of two that passes every check, selects the same either way (measured: without the switch it is selected the same and refused at structure, as with it).

- PACKAGE_ROW_K: the selection check that each member of a package supports the selected affordance tag group (row k)

- GP_COVERAGE_LENSES: a TEST-ONLY treatment (stage W1 (c1)), never the default: the chain's lens candidates and signal groups taken from Step 1's Game Problem Coverage row in both branches, so they no longer follow the wording
- CV-015: the sport module's Coverage row for GP-015: the candidate knowledge it records for that Game Problem (its lenses read under GP_COVERAGE_LENSES)
- Q-a2: our question to Christian, open since 10 November 2026: whether the kept checks that read lenses should read the wording's top three, any combination of the Game Problem's lenses, or every combination
- REVIEW_PATHWAY_K2: the constraints' authority where the chain takes the review pathway's K2 result in place of a per-goal authorization record (TEST-ONLY, stage W1 (b)): the empty set only, never relabelled AUTHORIZATION_RECORD
- K2_NOT_TAKEN: a verdict (stage W1 (b)): the review pathway proposed no manipulation, so its K2 result applies, and a TEST-ONLY record of ours stood in for it (the DRAFT-record comparison arm; a detected disconnection, never a pass)
- RC1.1: the version of the Representative Performance Context Library the program holds (its rows' last verified version)
- generateSelection.ts...: the constraint selector's source file, with line numbers; any other name ending .ts is an engine source file, with line numbers, and a short hexadecimal string after one (its origin) is a commit, found by git blame, that introduced the rule or the part of it named beside it
- TEST-ONLY: code, data or a treatment no route, controller or service reaches
- RESTATEMENT-ONLY: a refusal whose cited items are all ours and unreviewed (Christian's second reply of 7 November, section 2)
- APPROVED_TRANSLATION: Step 1's basis: the goal's approved translation row in the planning model
- SELECTION_TRACE_RANKING: where the chain's record of the forms its scorer ranked is read (its selection trace)
- AUTHORIZATION_RECORD: the constraints' authority: the authorization record's set (here a DRAFT record of ours, no ruling field filled)
- VOCABULARY_POOL: the constraints' authority the engine records where no authorization record governs: the goal's own candidate constraints (from its Game Problem's Coverage row, or the wording), which no record authorizes; nothing is drawn from outside them
- ENGINE_LENS: an affordance lens the engine scores from the goal's wording: an engine input, not knowledge
- NO_COMPATIBLE_COMBINATION: the selector's refusal: no combination of lenses and constraints passed its checks
- FOUNDATION_CLAUSE: the selection check that a non-empty package holds a foundation constraint (its keyword threshold decides which structure member is picked); since stage W2 bypassed only under FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS
- PACKAGE_ROW_O: the selection check that a package shows a clear relationship to the selected affordance (row o)
- CONSUMED: a verdict: the value equals the upstream one and the chain's own record shows the decision was made from it (the forms its scorer was handed; the record's pool)
- CARRIED_NOT_READ: a verdict: the chain carries the upstream value as a record that no decision of the chain reads (a detected disconnection, never a pass, never consumption)
- AGREES_INDEPENDENTLY: a verdict: equal values with no record of consumption (a latent disconnection, never counted as consumption)
- CONTRADICTED: a verdict: the value used downstream differs from the upstream one
- REFUSED_ON_OWN_CHOICE: a verdict: the chain refused on its own choice before consuming the upstream one
- ENGINE_RULE_DECIDED: a verdict: after the form was consumed, an engine rule refused the selection (a detected disconnection where attributed to a check kept under the authorization's section 5; a violation otherwise)
- CARRIED: a verdict: every open item constituting the clause reached derivation and lines of the built game rest on each, none of them without a value; no declaration replaced
- CARRIED_UNDER_TREATMENT: a verdict: the clause is carried only with a declaration replaced by a TEST-ONLY treatment (a detected disconnection, never a pass)
- CITED_NOT_CARRIED: a verdict: an approved row is cited (or displayed) while nothing carries it, or only part of it
- WITHHELD_BY_TREATMENT: a verdict: a declared TEST-ONLY treatment withheld it (a detected disconnection, never a pass)
- REFUSED_ON_CLAUSE_LINE: a verdict: the run's refusal cites a line of the clause's own items, so the clause was decided and stays explicitly unresolved (a detected disconnection, never a pass); read from the lines a refusal cites as c:<contract>:<item>::<row>; a later refusal naming the item by its value path leaves the clause's carriage verdict in place (item 2 says where)
- NOT_REACHED: a verdict: the run stopped before the decision
- NOT_CLASSIFIED: the requirement account's state for a scoring condition with no requirement bindings: never a pass
- NOT_AUTHORED: a restatement declaration: nothing authored the value
- UNDECLARED: a restatement declaration: whether a value is owed is not stated
- NO_FURTHER_VALUE: a declaration that a value needs no further referent: here only a TEST-ONLY treatment, not ruled
- OUTSIDE_BOUNDARY: an item recorded outside what the engine can check: it establishes nothing
- TEST ASSUMPTION: Christian's class 4: a stated test assumption, never knowledge
- UNAUTHORIZED_SOURCE: a verdict: constraints built from a source the authorization record does not authorize (the goal's own candidate constraints where no record authorizes them, VOCABULARY_POOL, or a constraint outside the record's set)
- violation verdict: a verdict that fails the assertion every experiment run must pass: AGREES_INDEPENDENTLY, CONTRADICTED, REFUSED_ON_OWN_CHOICE, UNAUTHORIZED_SOURCE, CITED_NOT_CARRIED, MISSING (a reached decision without its record), or ENGINE_RULE_DECIDED where no kept check is attributed; each one on the experiment path is listed as an open disconnection
- detected disconnection: a verdict printed as an open finding, never a pass and never a violation: CARRIED_NOT_READ, WITHHELD_BY_TREATMENT, CARRIED_UNDER_TREATMENT, REFUSED_ON_CLAUSE_LINE, K2_NOT_TAKEN, or ENGINE_RULE_DECIDED attributed to a kept check
- selection: the chain's stage that chooses the engine lenses and the constraints on the candidate forms (on the legacy route also the form); refused where no combination passes its checks
- knowledge: the stage after selection that checks the knowledge the build needs; in these runs, that the form, the context and each authorized constraint have a contribution contract
- structure: the stage that derives the game's configuration lines from the contracts and checks them; in these runs, a line that must hold a value and holds none refuses the run
- realization: the stage that closes the open lines with the realization choices, within their authored bounds
- validation: the stage that checks the realized game; in these runs, the Laws check
- communication: the last stage, the coach text; in these runs, a line with no approved wording, or that no sentence carries, refuses the run
- contribution contract: the engine-readable form of a knowledge row (a form, a context or a constraint), without which the row cannot shape a game
- row k: the selection check that each member of a package supports the selected affordance tag group (a package check, beside row o)
- zero branch: the selector's commitment of an evaluated, empty authorized set, before the size, role-mix and package checks (which then do not run)
- passed by: printed after a stop as (passed by X): the later path X gets past that stop
- inThisActivity: the chain's account field saying whether the context's approved condition is in this activity
The appendices are the detailed texts the A02 tests generate; they use these identifiers and the chain's own line, item and row identifiers, read as the key's c:restated: entry says.

1. CONSUMPTION OF STEP 1 AND STEP 2
Christian asked: "Whether the downstream pathway now consumes the authorized Game Problem and Game Form results without independent wording-based substitution." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): on the isolated pathway, yes. The chain takes Step 2's form as its form, its scorer handed only that form; Step 2 chooses the form from Step 1's Game Problem (with Step 1's primary swapped to GP-002, the chain takes GF7 as its form). Since stage W1 (a) (11 November 2026), Step 1's Game Problem also governs the chain's Game-Problem-dependent inputs, the lens hints and matched signals (CONSUMED; on GP-002 the lens candidates are GP-002's Coverage row's 7, while the planning model's reading GP-015 stays in the account beside it, never in its place). The context is still read from the routing (open, below). The production route is unchanged and still picks A02's form from the wording.
Implemented and tested:
- A TEST-ONLY option of the checked chain hands it Step 1's resolved Game Problem and Step 2's SELECTED form: its candidate forms become the routed context's declared forms filtered to Step 2's form (never replaced by it), a form outside them is refused, and its constraints come only from the authorization record or, since stage W1 (b), from the review pathway's K2 result in its place. Since stage W1 (a), Step 1's Game Problem is handed on to the guided selection hints (one TEST-ONLY option no route passes), which read it as the approved Game Problem; the chain records which Game Problem governed its hints and the hints its selector was handed.
- The 5 A02 runs (A02-0, A02-1, A02-1b, A02-2, A02-D): Step 1 RESOLVED GP-015 at RPC-002 (APPROVED_TRANSLATION); Step 2 SELECTED GF2 (compatible: GF2); the chain took GF2 as its form, its scorer handed only GF2; the handoff placed nothing.
- On 47 of the 48 experiment runs (every one that reaches the chain): the Game Form reads CONSUMED (47 runs), equal to Step 2's form, and the chain's own record shows its scorer was handed only it; the constraints read CONSUMED (47 runs): on the review pathway's K2 result (the empty set, no record of ours; 20 runs), or, on the runs of item 4, against the authorized set of a TEST-ONLY copy of our DRAFT A02 record (no ruling; the finding is ours), never against a decision of Step 1 or Step 2; Step 1's Game Problem reads CONSUMED (47 runs), and so does the Game Problem that governs the hints (47 runs): the chain's own record shows Step 1's value governed its lens hints and matched signals (before stage W1 (a) the Game Problem read CARRIED_NOT_READ, carried in the account and read by no chain decision). The one other run (Step 1's primary swapped to GP-001) is reconsidered by Step 2, which retains GF2, GF3, GF7 with no basis to select, and stops before the chain.
- Dependency: with Step 1's primary swapped to GP-002 in a test copy of the knowledge (TEST ASSUMPTION), Step 2 selects GF7 and the chain takes GF7 as its form, carrying GP-002, and is refused at knowledge (the game form GF7 has no contribution contract); with GF2's GP-015 cell moved to GF7 there, Step 2 selects GF7 and the chain takes GF7 as its form and is refused at knowledge (the game form GF7 has no contribution contract). The legacy route on the same two inputs stays on GF3 in both, its pick from the wording, and is refused at knowledge (the game form GF3 has no contribution contract).
- Wording: with the goal's wording rewritten twice (in the knowledge copy and in the row the chain reads; restored, the restore asserted), Steps 1 and 2 and the consumed form do not move (identical), while the chain's own route moves from GF3 to GF2, which proves the rewrite reached the chain.
Open (detected and listed as open disconnections, never passes):
- OD-CONTEXT: the context is read by the chain from the planning routing itself; no record of consuming Step 1's context exists (equal by agreement: both read the same routing row)
- OD-A02-LEGACY-FORM: the known A02 failure: the chain selects its own GF3 from the wording against Step 2's SELECTED form; closed on the experiment path by the consumption, open on the production route (unchanged)
- the engine lenses still follow the wording where Step 1's Game Problem and the wording agree (the wording's own pool is kept then), and on one two-manipulation set the wording decides the selection (item 3).
Taken since stage W1 (b) (11 November 2026), our reading, put to Christian (item 5): on the experiment path the checked pathway takes the review pathway's proposal of no manipulation under K2 in place of a per-goal authorization record (REVIEW_PATHWAY_K2, the empty set only, K2's limit carried into the account); the safeguards' ownership row now names every upstream result the chain can take, and the DRAFT-record comparison arm, where our TEST-ONLY record stands in for K2's result, reads K2_NOT_TAKEN (a detected disconnection, never a pass).
Proposals: none in this item. The Game Problem reading that governs the engine's lens hints is now taken from Step 1 on the isolated pathway (stage W1 (a)), as engineering under the package Christian delegated; OD-HINT-GAME-PROBLEM, listed open before, is closed.
Assumptions (OURS): the consumption is itself a TEST-ONLY treatment (TT-CONSUMED-STEPS); GF2's Game Problem cell keeps its grade, CANDIDATE.
Ecological suitability: not established; consuming GF2 shows structural compatibility only.

2. HIGH PRESS ESCAPE'S IDENTITY AND SCORING
Christian asked: "Whether the existing High Press Escape identity and scoring relationships can be carried into configuration, and what remains unsupported." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): partly. As the knowledge stands no game is built: A02-0 stops at structure on two clauses of the approved scoring condition, which enter derivation through our CANDIDATE restatement. A game is built only under labelled TEST-ONLY treatments of the condition (A02-1b, A02-2, A02-D); there the approved scoring events bound the event line, and the value within them is our realization choice, not knowledge. Nothing in a built game carries established pressure.
Implemented and tested:
- Our CANDIDATE restatement of RPC-002 in the TEST-ONLY draft store: 10 items, each quoting its approved row verbatim (the store's gate refuses a copy that does not); none of it is in production knowledge. Rows restated: RPC-REL-075, RPC-REL-076, RPC-REL-077, RPC-REL-078, RPC-PROP-133, RPC-ID-006, RPC-PROP-018, RPC-ID-008.
- Carried (only on A02-1b, A02-2, A02-D, each with TEST-ONLY treatments): the four approved events, narrowed with GF2's own to ['line_crossed', 'target_zone_entered'] on the built event line (the chain records our item as narrowing it); the value 'line_crossed' within that pair is a realization choice of ours (class 6), not knowledge. The values 'beyond' and 'retains possession through the next action' enter derivation.
- A02-0, the knowledge as it stands: refused at structure on c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-04.a::V5 (the control clause's referent) and c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-02.a::V6 (the origin clause's value): RESTATEMENT-ONLY: a conservative refusal by our CANDIDATE interpretation, not a finding that the configuration violates approved knowledge (Christian's second reply of 7 November, section 2).

3. THE SAFEGUARDS AGAINST REASONING DISCONNECTIONS
Christian asked: "Whether the proposed reasoning-disconnection safeguards detect the known A02 failure and comparable unauthorized substitutions." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): yes, for the known failure and for each substitution tested; they also detect disconnections that remain open, listed below and never counted as passes.
Implemented and tested (one pure check over each run's result, run by registered tests; no library, layer or monitoring system):
- Decision ownership: an ownership table names the owner, input, output and authority of each of 9 decisions (appendix B).
- The known failure: on L1 the chain refuses at selection on its own GF3 before consuming Step 2's GF2: REFUSED_ON_OWN_CHOICE; the assertion every experiment run must pass fails on it: 'ownership: L1: Game Form eligibility and choice owned by Step 2, under D3 over CANDIDATE cells: REFUSED_ON_OWN_CHOICE: the chain refused at selection on its own choice GF3 before consuming Step 2's GF2'.
- A comparable substitution: a test-only wrapper that drops the handed decisions, on a wording where the chain's own pick equals GF2: the form reads AGREES_INDEPENDENTLY and the assertion fails on it; the same run with the decisions handed reads CONSUMED and passes.
- Dependency verification: a changed Game Problem and a moved cell are reconsidered by Step 2, whose form the chain consumes (item 1); with Step 1 saying GP-002, the chain's hints follow GP-002 (CONSUMED: its lens candidates are GP-002's Coverage row's, the divergent arm of test 3, whose negative control, the option not handed on, leaves them the wording's), while the planning model's reading GP-015 stays in the account beside it (the Game Problem CONSUMED).
- Safeguard B fails on OD-WORDING-LENSES: the engine lenses (ENGINE_LENS) follow the goal's wording on the consumed path; no owned decision moved with them on these runs; before: Possession Stability Opportunity, Space Creation Opportunity, Regain Opportunity; after: Line-Breaking Opportunity, Possession Stability Opportunity, Space Creation Opportunity.
- Safeguard B fails on OD-WORDING-FLIP: wording alone decides the selection of a non-empty authorized set on the consumed form: Neutral Player with Turnover Reward is selected under the goal's wording and refused under a reworded one; before: the goal's wording: selected on GF2 with Neutral Player and Turnover Reward, then refused at knowledge (the authorized constraint Turnover Reward has no contribution contract); after: the reversed and the neutral wording: refused at selection, NO_COMPATIBLE_COMBINATION on GF2 (row k on every lens combination); lenses none; under the switch the authorized set is taken whole (stage W2), so the refusal rests on row k alone: a lone Neutral Player, which the package check admits on every lens combination of the reworded pool and which commits as S4-NP~reversed and S4-NP~neutral, is a different authorized set, never a subset the selector may choose.
- Consumption tracing: with one cited event removed from our restatement, A02-2 moves from communication to realization, on the event line that item shaped; with A02-D's treatment declaration removed, its three clauses read CITED_NOT_CARRIED and the assertion fails on it; with GP-015's CAR ratings changed, the chain's result is identical (consulted, not determining).
- Over the 81 runs: 156 violation verdicts and 102 detected disconnections, none of them a pass. Told apart by where the runs run:
   the experiment path (48 runs, each held to the assertion every experiment run must pass): 77 violation verdicts, every one a finding of an open disconnection listed below, counted once per run (OD-CONTEXT 47, OD-DISPLAY 30), and 76 detected disconnections (by verdict: ENGINE_RULE_DECIDED 16, CARRIED_UNDER_TREATMENT 4, WITHHELD_BY_TREATMENT 27, REFUSED_ON_CLAUSE_LINE 29);
   the controls (12 runs built beside the experiment to show whether a check bites, not held to that assertion; violation verdicts per run: A02-0/draft-record, A02-0 on the DRAFT-record comparison arm (our record in place of K2's result: K2_NOT_TAKEN), 2; A02-1/draft-record, A02-1 on the DRAFT-record comparison arm (our record in place of K2's result: K2_NOT_TAKEN), 2; A02-1b/draft-record, A02-1b on the DRAFT-record comparison arm (our record in place of K2's result: K2_NOT_TAKEN), 2; A02-2/draft-record, A02-2 on the DRAFT-record comparison arm (our record in place of K2's result: K2_NOT_TAKEN), 2; A02-D/draft-record, A02-D on the DRAFT-record comparison arm (our record in place of K2's result: K2_NOT_TAKEN), 2; S4-NP/no-bypass, the lone Neutral Player without the switch, 2; S4-NP+WZA/no-bypass, two manipulations without the switch, 2; S4-WZA/foundation-kept, WZA with foundation presence kept (the reversal arm of FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS: the foundation clause decides), 2; S4-TR/foundation-kept, TR with foundation presence kept (the reversal arm of FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS: the foundation clause decides), 2; S4-WZA+TR/foundation-kept, WZA+TR with foundation presence kept (the reversal arm of FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS: the foundation clause decides), 2; A02-2/CAR, A02-2 with GP-015's CAR ratings changed, 2; W-DROPS-UPSTREAM, the wrapper that drops the handed decisions (on the DRAFT-record arm), 6): 28 violation verdicts and 26 detected disconnections;
   the legacy route (21 runs, the production route and its variants, reported only): 51 violation verdicts and 0 detected disconnections.
Open disconnections (9; each with its evidence and runs in appendix B):
- OD-CONTEXT [experiment path]: the context is read by the chain from the planning routing itself; no record of consuming Step 1's context exists (equal by agreement: both read the same routing row)
- OD-DISPLAY [experiment path]: the account displays the approved scoring condition as in this activity (inThisActivity true) whenever the context's contract is among those the build reads, whether or not any clause is carried
- OD-PACKAGE-LENSES [experiment path]: after the form is consumed, a kept package check that reads the wording-derived lenses decides the selection (row o, row k and the other package checks on the members present, and every selected member validated)

- OD-LEGACY-VOCABULARY [legacy route, reported only]: constraints built from the goal's own candidate constraints, which no record authorizes (the engine labels their authority VOCABULARY_POOL; nothing is drawn from outside the goal's candidates)
- OD-LEGACY-GAME-PROBLEM [legacy route, reported only]: the legacy route reads its own Game Problem, which differs from Step 1's
- OD-A02-LEGACY-FORM [legacy route, reported only]: the known A02 failure: the chain selects its own GF3 from the wording against Step 2's SELECTED form; closed on the experiment path by the consumption, open on the production route (unchanged)
Taken since stage W1 (b) (11 November 2026), our reading, put to Christian (item 5): on the experiment path the checked pathway takes the review pathway's proposal of no manipulation under K2 in place of a per-goal authorization record (REVIEW_PATHWAY_K2, the empty set only, K2's limit carried into the account); the safeguards' ownership row now names every upstream result the chain can take, and the DRAFT-record comparison arm, where our TEST-ONLY record stands in for K2's result, reads K2_NOT_TAKEN (a detected disconnection, never a pass).

4. ZERO, ONE OR MORE MANIPULATIONS WITHOUT THE QUOTA
Christian asked: "Whether zero or one justified manipulation can be represented without the inherited numerical quota interfering." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): zero, one, two or three manipulations can be represented without a quota interfering (stage W2, 11 November 2026): under the TEST-ONLY switch the record's authorized set is taken whole, whatever its size, its pool uncapped, every selected member validated, and, under the declared TEST-ONLY treatment FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS, with no foundation member required; each run is selected whole (S4-NP, S4-NP+WZA, S4-NP+TR, S4-WZA+TR, S4-NP+WZA+TR) or refused by a named kept check, never by the size rule, foundation presence, the role mix or the bounded search. No manipulation is justified for A02 in anything we hold, so what is shown is the representation, not a justified configuration.
Implemented and tested:
- Zero: the 5 A02 runs commit the review pathway's K2 result, the empty set (constraints CONSUMED, none built, since stage W1 (b)): representable, not shown to be justified; K2's limit travels with the result into the account, "Retaining a Game Form does not establish that it is optimal or sufficient for the Learning Goal." (received 7 November 2026, first reply). On the DRAFT-record comparison arm the same runs commit our DRAFT record's empty set (K2_NOT_TAKEN), with the same outcome, stage and reasons; the record's finding is ours: no manipulation has an authorized relationship to RPC-002 and GP-015 in held knowledge.
- One: a lone Neutral Player in the DRAFT record's set (a TEST-ONLY relationship stand-in, TEST ASSUMPTION) is selected with the TEST-ONLY size-rule bypass (S4-NP: selected, then refused at structure, on the same lines as A02-0) and refused without it (S4-NP/no-bypass: refused at selection, attributed to the size rule): the quota interfered, and the bypass removed only it.
- One, refused by kept checks (stage W2, under FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS): a lone Wide Zone Advantage is first refused as one-sided (the other package checks), a lone Turnover Reward by row k on one lens combination and row o on the others, Central Density Condition, alone and with Interception Reward, by the package relationship check (row o); each attributed by re-running the selector's own exported package check on GF2 and each lens combination with the run's options, which returns the first reason it meets (S4-WZA PACKAGE_OTHER, S4-TR PACKAGE_ROW_K and PACKAGE_ROW_O, S4-CDC PACKAGE_ROW_O, S4-CDC+IR PACKAGE_ROW_O).
- Two: Neutral Player with Wide Zone Advantage is selected whole, then refused at structure, and without the switch it selects the same (S4-NP+WZA/no-bypass); with Turnover Reward it is selected, then refused at knowledge (Turnover Reward has no contribution contract); Wide Zone Advantage with Turnover Reward, no foundation member, is selected whole, then refused at knowledge; Neutral Player with Central Density Condition, two members of one role, is refused by MEMBER_PRESENCE (the package holds one member per role, so Central Density Condition would never be validated).
- Three: Neutral Player, Wide Zone Advantage and Turnover Reward are selected whole, then refused at knowledge; Neutral Player, Wide Zone Advantage and Central Density Condition are refused whole by MEMBER_PRESENCE, while the pair Neutral Player with Wide Zone Advantage passes every lens combination: with the sizes as they were (2 to 4 for a set of three) the selector would have committed that pair, a choice no record made (the negative control of the set whole: such a strict subset built reads UNAUTHORIZED_SOURCE).
- The set whole (stage W2): under the switch the only size tried is the set's own (generateSelection.ts:1047), so the scorer chooses the lenses only, never how many or which of the record's constraints. Under the reworded wordings Neutral Player with Turnover Reward is refused at selection by row k on every lens combination (S4-NP+TR~reversed, S4-NP+TR~neutral); a lone Neutral Player, selected as S4-NP~reversed and S4-NP~neutral, is another authorized set, never a subset the selector may choose.
- The pool whole: the bounded search's cap of 2 per role is not applied to the record's pool under the switch (a probe on the package check shows a set of three members of one role reaching it whole on every lens combination, and never without the switch). Every selected member validated: a set any member of which is absent from the checked package is refused, never committed unvalidated.
- Every line a manipulation brings rests on its stand-in, class 4 (TEST ASSUMPTION) at best: 7 lines for Neutral Player alone, 25 with Wide Zone Advantage (on the diagnostic base, where a game is built).
- Of the 16 selection checks, under the switch the size rule with its floor and the bounded search over constraints are bypassed and every selected member is validated (a tightening); foundation presence is bypassed only under its declared treatment, which Christian can reverse by its removal (the reversal arm, S4-WZA/foundation-kept, S4-TR/foundation-kept, S4-WZA+TR/foundation-kept: refused by the foundation clause on every lens combination); every other check runs and enforces wherever its path is reached. On the empty-set runs the role mix, the package checks, the size rule, the bounded search and the tightening are not run at all: the zero branch commits the empty set first, and its authority is Christian's general principle of 15 October 2026 (a constraint is not mandatory unless justified).
- Reported with source and authority, as Christian asked: the role mix: source generateSelection.ts:591-613, applied :844; origin 3980188, 3 May 2026 (the rule, :591-613); dc98ecb, 14 October 2026 (its needs read over the goal's pool, :824-832, applied :844); authority none found for the rule; the pool-relative reading of its needs is our reading of Christian's 14 October ruling (constraints only from the goal's designated candidates), not a ruling on the role mix; kept and enforced, not treated as canonical.
- Reported with source and authority, as Christian asked: the foundation clause: a non-empty package holds a foundation (structure-role) member: source build-constraint-package.ts:634-641; validate-constraint-package.ts:333-335; origin e60a740, 18 April 2026 (the build-constraint-package.ts lines); dc98ecb, 14 October 2026 (validate-constraint-package.ts: the shaping half removed after Christian's 14 October ruling, the foundation half kept, not ruled); authority none found; engine rule (our own comment calls it not ruled); bypassed only under its declared TEST-ONLY treatment (our classification, reversible), not treated as canonical.

- On the production route (reported only, not changed): the package check holds one member per role and drops a consequence scoring 12 or less, so a selection of four, or of two members of one role, commits a member it never validated; measured on the production selections, 10 goals commit such a member (A03: central-density-condition; A04: zone-structure-condition; A05: turnover-reward; A06: goalkeeper-included-condition; D01: central-density-condition; D02: interception-reward; TA01: interception-reward; TA02: turnover-reward; TD01: transition-trigger; TD02: central-density-condition, delay-reward).
Open: the wording decides one two-manipulation selection (item 3), by row k alone now that the set is taken whole. Whether a record may ever be a menu to choose among, and on what learning-design basis, is Christian's question Q-a1 (until it is answered the set is taken whole).
Proposals (OURS, for Christian's decision): whether the TEST-ONLY switch in the constraint selector may stay (the disclosure above); it is removable. Our classification of foundation presence as a quota, reversible by removing FOUNDATION_PRESENCE_BYPASS. Whether the production route's unvalidated members (above) should be refused as the switch refuses them: a production change, not made here.
Assumptions (OURS): every manipulation here is a TEST-ONLY stand-in, never knowledge; foundation presence classified as a quota.
Ecological suitability: not established: selected means only that the kept checks did not refuse it.

6. REUSE BETWEEN A02 AND TA02 (brief)
Christian asked: "Whether the existing knowledge relationships appear capable of supporting reasoning reuse between A02 and TA02, without requiring a new goal-family structure." (reply about A02, pasted 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): Measured, the Game Problem and Game Form reasoning is shared without any new structure: both goals resolve to GP-015 through their own approved translation rows, and Step 2 selects GF2 through GF2's same GP-015 cell (graded CANDIDATE in both), each over its own context's declared-form rows; the same TEST-ONLY consumption carries both results to the chain. On the affordance side only the CAR profile Step 1 consults is shared, and it determines nothing downstream; the engine lenses, scored from each goal's wording, differ, and each context's affordance targets stay deferred. What would set the two contexts apart lives in each context's own rows (its identity, its opposition and its scoring condition), which the chain applies after the shared reasoning, through each context's restatement: the existing architecture already has that place. It does not execute yet: as the knowledge stands both goals stop at structure on their own restatements, A02 on its control clause's referent and its origin clause's value, TA02 on its origin clause's value alone; TA02's control items, which restate the possession condition of a clause Christian wrote as an example, have been held as examples since the merge of the program branch (11 November 2026), as Christian's first reply of 7 November, section 4, asked: “Similarly, do not convert "maintaining attacking possession" into a separate mandatory scoring requirement without identifying an authoritative basis.” (received 7 November 2026, first reply). With every condition item withheld the two built activities collapse into one plain GF2 activity (identical coach text; identical configuration lines but one transition value's source). So the existing relationships appear capable of the reuse Christian describes; what keeps the two contexts from collapsing is the missing operational interpretation and carrier of each context's condition, not a missing goal-family structure.
Implemented and tested: TA02 run through the same consumption (TA02-0, the knowledge as it stands; TA02-D, the diagnostic), over our DRAFT restatement of RPC-003 (TEST-ONLY draft store) and, as for A02, the review pathway's K2 result in place of a per-goal record (the DRAFT TA02 record set aside): its Game Problem reads CONSUMED, its Game Form reads CONSUMED, its constraints read CONSUMED. Measured from both goals' results (appendix C):
- shared (9): Step 1: the resolved Game Problem; Step 1: the CAR profile it consults; Step 2: the Game Form result; Step 2: the grade of GF2's Game Problem cell; the consumption (what the chain records it was handed); the approved scoring events (our restatement, as a set); the built scoring event (diagnostic runs); the coach text (diagnostic runs); the laws owed an adaptation (diagnostic runs).

UNRESOLVED (10; kept explicit, never closed by an assumption; the same list stands on every run)
- UN-ORIGIN [UNRESOLVED] the origin clause of the approved condition (RPC-PROP-133): the sequence begins under established coordinated pressure: no carrier: the only typed begin condition is a restart (register V5: the begin condition of a restart, a STOP_RESUME transition), the only episode scope is BUILD_OUT_EPISODE, RPC-001's (SD-36: Christian's approval of 20 September of that scope, the attacking episode that begins with RPC-001's build-out), and V5 excludes a player's position during play; our restatement carries the clause as RPC-002-02.a with its referent as open text (RPC-002-02.b) and its value declared NOT_AUTHORED, and the knowledge run is refused on that line (c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-02.a::V6)
- UN-PRESSING-LINE [UNRESOLVED] the referent of the progression clause: the pressing line: open text in our restatement (RPC-002-03.b); RPC-002 authors no marked pressing line, the defenders' line in play is a player position (excluded from V5), and typing it as the target the scoring team attacks would replace the pressing line by the target, which the C54 distinction keeps apart (C54: Christian's ruling that A01's progression reference and its pressure reference are two distinct references); not typed by us. The value 'beyond' is carried (RPC-002-03.c)
- UN-CONTROL-REFERENT [UNRESOLVED] the referent of the control clause: retains possession through the next action: not stated: UNDECLARED in our restatement, so the knowledge run and A02-1 are refused on c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-04.a::V5; A02-1b and A02-2 pass that line only under the TEST-ONLY declaration NO_FURTHER_VALUE (TT-CONTROL-NO-FURTHER-VALUE: the analog of C55, Christian's ruling that an A01 build-out's origin needs no further value; not ruled for A02; would need Christian's approval under M6). The value is carried (RPC-002-04.b)
- UN-SCOPE [UNRESOLVED] the scope of the condition: every condition item is scoped WHOLE_GAME (OURS), which holds every score of the game to the condition; the faithful scope is a High Press Escape sequence, which no vocabulary holds (an episode scope is added only by Christian's approval, as SD-36, Christian's approval of 20 September, added BUILD_OUT_EPISODE)
- UN-WORDING [UNRESOLVED] coach wording for the condition: none approved: A02-2 is refused at communication, awaiting approved wording for the control condition's type and value
- UN-RESTART-POSSESSION [UNRESOLVED] the restart and change-of-possession half of the starting state: SD-14 (Christian's decision of 18 September) makes a change of possession begin a new attacking episode, and defines episode boundaries only; Christian's caution of 8 November says a restart, a change of possession and a new attack are not automatically equivalent; GF2-17 makes play continue on a change of possession. Features of GF2 bearing on established pressure: possession changes continue (GF2-17), and each team attacks the end opposite its own (GF2-08.c, GF2-08.e: the per-team directional arrangement, by Christian's ruling of 9 October), the same for both teams; so nothing in GF2 makes one team the pressing team (OURS: no ruling we hold addresses pressing in GF2). - the Game Problem: owner Step 1; input the goal id, the approved translation or a scoped ruling, the routing; output the resolved primary Game Problem, carried by the chain beside its own reading and read by the chain's hints (the next row); authority the approved translation, or the scoped ruling it rests on

- the Representative Performance Context: owner Step 1; input the routing of the planning model; output the context whose approved rows govern scoring; authority the routing; the chain reads it itself (no consumption record exists for it)
- Game Form eligibility and choice: owner Step 2, under D3 over CANDIDATE cells; input Step 1, the context form rows, the form Game Problem cells, D3; output SELECTED, retained alternatives or none; the chain's candidate forms filtered to it (an intersection); authority Step 2 under D3; each cell keeps its own grade (a CANDIDATE cell is never printed as approved, preferred or sufficient)
- the constraints (manipulations): owner the upstream result the chain takes, one of: Christian's authorization record (AUTHORIZATION_RECORD); the review pathway's K2 result where it proposes no manipulation (REVIEW_PATHWAY_K2, the empty set only, review-only, since stage W1 (b)); or a TEST-ONLY record whose finding is ours (AUTHORIZATION_RECORD, printed distinctly); input the record's authorized set (the record route), or K2's result with its limit (in place of a per-goal record, never beside one); where the review pathway proposes no manipulation and a TEST-ONLY record of ours stood in, K2_NOT_TAKEN; output the selection's candidate pool and the constraints built; authority Christian's ruling where Christian's record governs; K2 (approved for review-only use, received 7 November 2026) with E5 (received 3 November 2026), our reading, where its result is taken; none where the record is a TEST-ONLY one of ours
- whether the selection commits: owner the owned decisions above; an engine rule may refuse only as a check kept under the authorization's section 5; input the consumed form, the authorized set and the engine lenses; output a committed selection, or a refusal with the rule that refused; authority none found for the engine rules (reported with source and authority)
- the context's approved identity and scoring, clause by clause: owner the context's approved rows, through our CANDIDATE restatement; input the restated items that constitute each clause of the cited approved row; output the clause carried into the built game, withheld by a declared treatment, or not carried; authority the approved row; the restatement is ours and unreviewed
- what the account displays about the approved condition: owner the same approved row; input the account's context display; output whether the account says the condition is in this activity; authority the approved row; a display is never a pass
- realization choices and justified defaults: owner the chain, legitimately; input the consumed contracts and the realization choices; output the configuration lines, the text and the diagram; authority derives; closes open lines within authored bounds (graded line by line by the handoff)

APPENDIX D: THE CASES AS THE HANDOFF REPORTS THEM (the A02 runs and the TA02 runs: inputs, Steps 1 and 2, the form, the chain, configuration lines, coach text, laws and requirement accounts, refusals and what they rest on)
HANDOFF REPORT: the isolated reusable configuration handoff (TEST-ONLY review evidence)

What this is: the review pathway's Steps 1 and 2 handed to the checked configuration and rendering chain, case by case, with every line and sentence graded by what determined it. Engineering evidence only: passing these tests is not evidence of ecological suitability or of representative learning quality in the field. Nothing here is served to a coach, written to production knowledge, or an authorization.
Every configuration below: Productive, recurring opportunities to meet the intended Game Problem: not established (no approved relationship ties a Game Problem to any line).
How to read the coach text (OURS): 'wording APPROVED' means the sentence is, word for word, one of Christian's approved coach sentences; it says nothing about the standing of what it states (its class). 'wording DRAFT' means words that are not approved (the renderer's DRAFT wording, or the review-only wording of decision 1).
The renderer's own status words: DERIVED, required by the selected knowledge and worded as a requirement of the game; STARTING, where this activity starts (a value chosen inside authored bounds, an alternative taken, or a preferred default), said as a fact about this activity and adjustable; COACH_CHOICE, left to the coach and put to the coach as a choice.
Quotation marks: double, Christian's words, verbatim, with their received date (or, for a reply pasted without its mail header, its paste date); single, ours (labels, values, record text such as an item's evidence, and engine sentences, whose own double quotation marks are printed as single ones).
Diagram words (the labels of the diagram's dimensions, targets and teams) are compared and printed with their status, CHRISTIANS_TERM (the diagram's value for one of Christian's terms, printed under this token) or DRAFT, and the sentence of the coach text that carries them, where one does.

Christian's six classes:
   class 1: Approved canonical knowledge.
   class 2: An explicit scoped ruling.
   class 3: Candidate or provisional knowledge.
   class 4: A stated test assumption.
   class 5: An illustrative configuration choice.
   class 6: An unsupported or unresolved relationship.

The grading map (OURS, for Christian's review): each line takes the weakest class among its determining premises; each premise is printed with its own standing and, for a restatement, its source's standing beside it.
   RESTATED_ITEM: CANDIDATE; printed 'our restatement of {source}'; a contract item we restated (basis AUTHORED) from a source; the source's own standing is printed beside it, never this item's
   KNOWLEDGE_RULING_ITEM: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a knowledge item with basis OWNER_RULING (class 3 where the ruling is provisional)
   ASSUMED_ITEM: OURS; printed 'OURS: assumption in our restatement'; a contract item with basis ASSUMED; what rests on it inherits it
   ENGINE_ONLY_ITEM: OURS; printed 'OURS: engine convention'; a contract item with basis ENGINE_ONLY
   DRAFT_ITEM: CANDIDATE; printed 'DRAFT knowledge (ours, unreviewed)'; an item of a DRAFT contract read from the TEST-ONLY draft store
   REALIZATION_ITEM: RULING; printed 'RULING'; an item of a provisional realization quoting Christian, graded by its ruling (class 3 where provisional)
   STANDING_DECISION: RULING; printed 'RULING'; an engine standing decision that records a ruling of Christian's (the table is data, checked against the specification); one that records none is OURS: engine convention
   CASE_CHOICE_RULING: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a choice the case supplies for an open line, on a ruling of Christian's (class 3 where provisional)
   CASE_CHOICE_ILLUSTRATIVE: ILLUSTRATIVE; printed 'ILLUSTRATIVE (for this offline test only; to be replaced before any production use)'; a choice the case supplies for an open line, illustrative
   FILE_CHOICE_RULING: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a choice from a realization-choices file whose entries quote Christian (class 3 where provisional)
   FILE_CHOICE_OURS: OURS; printed 'OURS: a realization choice of ours, not knowledge'; a choice from a realization-choices file that says it is a realizer's input, not knowledge
   COACH: no standing; printed 'the coach decides'; a line the chain leaves to the coach (closure COACH_DECIDES): no class claimed
   ASSUMED_BOUND: OURS; printed 'OURS: assumption in our restatement'; the authored bound a justified default closes a line within, where that bound is ASSUMED
   STAND_IN: no standing; printed '{declared}'; a TEST-ONLY stand-in item, graded by the label its input declares (ILLUSTRATIVE class 5, TEST ASSUMPTION class 4), never by the item's own basis
   SESSION_ILLUSTRATIVE: ILLUSTRATIVE; printed 'ILLUSTRATIVE (for this offline test only; to be replaced before any production use)'; a session input entered offline
   SESSION_COACH_INPUT: RULING; printed 'coach input, within a ruled bound'; a coach's session input within ruled testing boundaries: the bound's ruling gives the class
   LAWS_DEFAULT_PRINCIPLE: RULING; printed 'RULING'; Christian's standing principle that the Laws apply by default
   LAWS_ENCODING: CANDIDATE; printed 'DRAFT: our per-law encoding'; what each law requires here, as our DRAFT encoding says
   LAW_DEPARTURE: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a departure from a law recorded on a provisional realization, by its ruling (class 3 where provisional)
   FORM_SELECTED: no standing; printed '{weakest of Step 2}'; the form Step 2 SELECTED, graded by the weakest standing among what decided it
   FORM_ILLUSTRATIVE: ILLUSTRATIVE; printed 'ILLUSTRATIVE (for this offline test only; to be replaced before any production use)'; a form the case names as illustrative, permitted by a filed ruling and checked among the compatible forms
   FORM_INFERENCE: OURS; printed 'OURS: LOGICAL INFERENCE (our reading, not canonical; needs Christian's confirmation)'; a form the case names on our logical inference, checked among the compatible forms
   UNRESOLVED: UNRESOLVED; printed 'UNRESOLVED: no authorized value; needs a knowledge decision'; a line no premise determines; the missing basis is named

Decision kinds A, B and C (OURS, for Christian's review; computed per line by this rule, with the case's contextual overrides):
   A where choosing differently, within what the form and the Laws permit, would materially change the affordance landscape, opposition, scoring consequences, challenge or representative information: here, a line chosen among alternatives, a default that has an alternative, a law departed from, or a value carried by a stand-in;
   B where the value is entailed by the selected form, the Sport Profile, an engine standing decision or the Laws' default, or is a necessary adaptation (still scrutinized, never exempt);
   C where several values are admissible and none changes the above: here, a line the chain leaves to the coach; any functional bound on it stays B;
   session players, dimensions and duration are contextual A by the case's overrides; a line nothing establishes is no configuration value and has no kind.
The examination of the existing structures (OURS, for Christian's review):
   What the existing structures already mark, per line: where a value may come from (the register's source kinds and fill permissions); its authority (Christian's six classes; a contract item's basis); how binding it is (strictness, value status, a derived line's strength); who decides it (the chain's coach-decided closure; a record's authorized adjustments); what the coach must be told (the overview essentials table); and, for the Laws only, whether an owed adaptation is material (our DRAFT classification).
   None of them alone separates the three kinds: a coach-adjustable line can be learning-governing, a structurally checked part can be learning-governing, a material law adaptation merges meaning with structural validity, and the illustrative class holds all three kinds.
   What suffices without a new library or layer: one field in the handoff's output (each line's kind, computed by the rule above from the markers the chain already writes) and per-case overrides in the review data where the boundary is contextual. Routine choices (bib colours, spare balls, marking equipment, the coach's position, delivery details) are not configuration values here: the handoff holds no value, no ruling and no line for them, and leaves them to the coach.
